## 2. FLUX DE DONNÉES COMPLET

### 2.1 Flux Principal: OSINT → Intelligence → UI

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  FLOW 1: OSINT INGESTION → GRAPH → AI → ALERT → UI                        │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

[1] SOURCE EXTERNE (RSS, API, Honeypot)
     │
     ▼
[2] OSINT INGEST (8001)
     │  • Collecte données brutes
     │  • Parse + Normalize
     │  • Extract IOCs
     │
     ▼ Publish: osiris.ingest.osint.received
     
[3] ENTITY RESOLUTION (8002)
     │  • Subscribe: osiris.ingest.*
     │  • Dedup + Merge entities
     │  • Create canonical entity
     │
     ▼ Publish: osiris.enrichment.entity.resolved
         Publish: osiris.graph.entity.created
     
[4] PARALLEL ENRICHMENT
     │
     ├─► GEOINT ENRICHER (8003)
     │   • GeoIP lookup
     │   • Reverse DNS
     │   • ASN lookup
     │
     ├─► IOC MATCHER (8004)
     │   • Threat intel feeds
     │   • VirusTotal, AbuseIPDB
     │   • Reputation scoring
     │
     └─► AI ENRICHER (8005)
         • LLM contextualization
         • Entity linking
     
     ▼ Merge: osiris.enrichment.ai.completed
     
[5] GRAPH ENGINE (8006)
     │  • Subscribe: osiris.enrichment.*
     │  • MERGE nodes + edges
     │  • Update attributes
     │  • Compute risk scores
     │
     ▼ Publish: osiris.graph.entity.updated
     
[6] AI SYNTHESIZER (8007)
     │  • Subscribe: osiris.graph.*
     │  • Pattern detection
     │  • Campaign inference
     │  • Anomaly detection
     │
     ▼ Publish: osiris.ai.prediction.computed
         Publish: osiris.ai.anomaly.detected
     
[7] ALERT GENERATOR (8011)
     │  • Subscribe: osiris.ai.*
     │  • Rules engine
     │  • Severity scoring
     │
     ▼ Publish: osiris.intelligence.alert.created
     
[8] WORKFLOW ENGINE (8008)
     │  • Subscribe: osiris.intelligence.*
     │  • Execute playbooks
     │  • Create cases
     │  • Assign analysts
     │
     ▼ Publish: osiris.workflow.started
     
[9] WEBSOCKET GATEWAY (8009)
     │  • Subscribe: osiris.* (all)
     │  • Filter per workspace
     │  • Transform → UI messages
     │
     ▼ Push: WebSocket (real-time)
     
[10] FRONTEND (3000)
      • EntityGraphPanel (react-force-graph)
      • IntelligenceFeed (alerts)
      • WorkflowRunner (status)
      • Real-time updates via WS
```

### 2.2 Flux Secondaires

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  FLOW 2: INVESTIGATION (Analyst Action)                                    │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

ANALYST (Frontend)
     │
     ▼ Click: "Expand Entity"
[1] Frontend → API: GET /api/entity/expand?type=IP&id=192.168.1.1
     │
     ▼
[2] API Route → Graph Engine (8006)
     │  • Query: MATCH (e {id})-[r]-(target) RETURN target, r
     │  • Cache: Redis (hot queries)
     │
     ▼ Response: {nodes: [...], edges: [...]}
     
[3] Frontend → Render Graph
     │  • react-force-graph
     │  • Animation
     │  • Click → Expand again
     
[4] OPTIONAL: Auto-enrichment
     │  • Publish: osiris.graph.entity.expand_requested
     │  • Trigger: enrichment pipeline
     │  • Push: graph.update via WS


┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  FLOW 3: THREAT INVESTIGATION (AI-Assisted)                                │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

ANALYST: "Investigate Campaign X"
     │
     ▼
[1] Frontend → AI Engine (8007)
     │  • Request: "Analyze campaign X, find related entities"
     │  • Context: Graph subgraph + OSINT data
     │
     ▼
[2] AI Engine
     │  • RAG: Retrieve relevant graph context
     │  • LLM: Generate investigation plan
     │  • Graph AI: Predict related entities
     │
     ▼ Response: {plan: [...], predictions: [...], confidence: 0.87}
     
[3] Frontend → Display
     │  • Show AI recommendations
     │  • Highlight predicted entities
     │  • Suggest next actions


┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  FLOW 4: REAL-TIME MONITORING (Event-Driven UI)                            │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

EVENT BUS
     │
     ├─► osiris.intelligence.alert.created [CRITICAL]
     │   └─► WebSocket Gateway → Frontend
     │       └─► Display: "New Critical Alert" (toast + sidebar)
     │
     ├─► osiris.graph.entity.updated [risk_score: 95]
     │   └─► WebSocket Gateway → Frontend
     │       └─► Update: Graph node color (red)
     │
     └─► osiris.workflow.step.completed
         └─► WebSocket Gateway → Frontend
             └─► Update: Workflow progress bar
```

## 3. CHOIX TECHNOLOGIQUES

### 3.1 Justifications

| Couche | Technologie | Justification |
|--------|-------------|---------------|
| **Event Bus** | Redpanda | Kafka-compatible, 2-10x plus rapide, déploiement simplifié, tiered storage |
| **Graph DB** | Memgraph | Natif Cypher, temps réel, OSINT-native, performances supérieures à Neo4j |
| **Vector DB** | Qdrant | Open-source, performant, filtres scalaires, multi-tenant |
| **LLM** | Ollama | Local-first, privacy, pas de dépendance cloud, modèles open-source |
| **Time-Series** | ClickHouse | OLAP, compression, requêtes analytiques rapides |
| **Cache** | Redis | Latence < 1ms, structures riches, pub/sub |
| **Search** | OpenSearch | Full-text, filtres, agrégations, Kibana |
| **Frontend** | Next.js 14 | SSR/SSG, App Router, API routes, écosystème mature |
| **Graph UI** | react-force-graph-2d | Performance, force-directed, WebGL, interactivité |
| **Map** | MapLibre GL | Open-source, WebGL, tuiles vectorielles |
| **Backend** | Go | Performance, concurrence, typage, déploiement binaire |
| **Python** | FastAPI | AI/ML ecosystem, async, auto-documentation |
| **Container** | Docker | Standard, isolation, portabilité |
| **Orchestration** | Docker Compose | Simplicité, suffisant pour scale < 10 services |
| **Gateway** | Nginx | Performance, WebSocket, reverse proxy, load balancing |
| **Observability** | Prometheus + Loki + Tempo | Stack standard, intégration Grafana, OpenTelemetry |

### 3.2 Pourquoi PAS [X]?

| Technologie écartée | Raison |
|---------------------|--------|
| **Neo4j** | Memgraph plus rapide, licence différente, meilleur pour temps réel |
| **Kafka (Java)** | Redpanda plus simple, plus rapide, même API |
| **Neptune (AWS)** | Vendor lock-in, coût, pas de contrôle |
| **Elasticsearch** | OpenSearch plus open-source, licence Apache 2.0 |
| **React** | Next.js inclut React + SSR + routing |
| **D3.js** | react-force-graph plus performant pour graphes dynamiques |
| **Kubernetes** | Overkill pour < 20 services, Docker Compose suffit |
| **RabbitMQ** | Kafka/Redpanda meilleur pour event streaming |
| **PostgreSQL** | ClickHouse meilleur pour time-series, Memgraph pour graphes |

## 5. DÉPLOIEMENT

### 5.1 Docker Compose Principal

```yaml
# docker-compose.yml

networks:
  osiris-net:
    driver: bridge
    ipam:
      config:
        - subnet: 172.20.0.0/16

volumes:
  redpanda-data:
  memgraph-data:
  clickhouse-data:
  opensearch-data:
  qdrant-data:
  redis-data:
  prometheus-data:
  grafana-data:
  loki-data:
  tempo-data:

services:
  # ─── Event Bus ─────────────────────────────────────────────
  redpanda:
    image: docker.redpanda.com/redpandadata/redpanda:v24.1.4
    command:
      - redpanda
      - start
      - --kafka-addr internal://0.0.0.0:9092,external://0.0.0.0:19092
      - --advertise-kafka-addr internal://redpanda:9092,external://localhost:19092
    ports:
      - "19092:19092"
    volumes:
      - redpanda-data:/var/lib/redpanda/data
    networks: [osiris-net]

  # ─── Data Stores ───────────────────────────────────────────
  memgraph:
    image: memgraph/memgraph:2.18.1
    volumes:
      - memgraph-data:/var/lib/memgraph
    networks: [osiris-net]
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "echo 'RETURN 1;' | mgconsole"]
      interval: 10s

  clickhouse:
    image: clickhouse/clickhouse-server:24.6-alpine
    volumes:
      - clickhouse-data:/var/lib/clickhouse
    networks: [osiris-net]

  opensearch:
    image: opensearchproject/opensearch:2.11.0
    environment:
      - discovery.type=single-node
      - plugins.security.disabled=true
    volumes:
      - opensearch-data:/usr/share/opensearch/data
    networks: [osiris-net]

  qdrant:
    image: qdrant/qdrant:v1.7.0
    volumes:
      - qdrant-data:/qdrant/storage
    networks: [osiris-net]

  redis:
    image: redis:7.2-alpine
    command: redis-server --appendonly yes
    volumes:
      - redis-data:/data
    networks: [osiris-net]

  # ─── Backend Services ──────────────────────────────────────
  osiris-ingest:
    build:
      context: ./services/osint-ingest
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
    environment:
      - REDIS_URL=redis://redis:6379
      - KAFKA_BROKERS=redpanda:9092
    depends_on:
      - redis
      - redpanda
    networks: [osiris-net]

  entity-resolution:
    build:
      context: ./services/entity-resolution
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
    environment:
      - MEMGRAPH_HOST=memgraph
      - REDIS_URL=redis://redis:6379
      - KAFKA_BROKERS=redpanda:9092
    depends_on:
      - memgraph
      - redis
      - redpanda
    networks: [osiris-net]

  geoint-enricher:
    build:
      context: ./services/geoint-enricher
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
    environment:
      - REDIS_URL=redis://redis:6379
      - KAFKA_BROKERS=redpanda:9092
    depends_on:
      - redis
      - redpanda
    networks: [osiris-net]

  ioc-matcher:
    build:
      context: ./services/ioc-matcher
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
    environment:
      - REDIS_URL=redis://redis:6379
      - KAFKA_BROKERS=redpanda:9092
    depends_on:
      - redis
      - redpanda
    networks: [osiris-net]

  graph-engine:
    build:
      context: ./services/graph-engine
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
    environment:
      - MEMGRAPH_HOST=memgraph
      - REDIS_URL=redis://redis:6379
      - KAFKA_BROKERS=redpanda:9092
    depends_on:
      - memgraph
      - redis
      - redpanda
    networks: [osiris-net]

  ai-synthesizer:
    build:
      context: ./services/ai-synthesizer
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
    environment:
      - OLLAMA_HOST=ollama:11434
      - QDRANT_HOST=qdrant
      - REDIS_URL=redis://redis:6379
      - KAFKA_BROKERS=redpanda:9092
    depends_on:
      - qdrant
      - redis
      - redpanda
    networks: [osiris-net]

  alert-generator:
    build:
      context: ./services/alert-generator
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
    environment:
      - REDIS_URL=redis://redis:6379
      - KAFKA_BROKERS=redpanda:9092
    depends_on:
      - redis
      - redpanda
    networks: [osiris-net]

  workflow-engine:
    build:
      context: ./services/workflow-engine
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
    environment:
      - REDIS_URL=redis://redis:6379
      - KAFKA_BROKERS=redpanda:9092
    depends_on:
      - redis
      - redpanda
    networks: [osiris-net]

  websocket-gateway:
    build:
      context: ./services/websocket-gateway
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
    ports:
      - "8009:8009"
    environment:
      - KAFKA_BROKERS=redpanda:9092
    depends_on:
      - redpanda
    networks: [osiris-net]

  control-plane:
    build:
      context: ./services/control-plane
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
    ports:
      - "8010:8010"
    environment:
      - REDIS_URL=redis://redis:6379
      - KAFKA_BROKERS=redpanda:9092
    depends_on:
      - redis
      - redpanda
    networks: [osiris-net]

  # ─── Frontend ──────────────────────────────────────────────
  osiris-app:
    build:
      context: ./frontend
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
    ports:
      - "3000:3000"
    environment:
      - NODE_ENV=production
      - WS_URL=ws://websocket-gateway:8009
    depends_on:
      - websocket-gateway
    networks: [osiris-net]

  # ─── Gateway ───────────────────────────────────────────────
  osiris-nginx-gateway:
    build:
      context: ./gateway
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
    ports:
      - "3100:3100"
    environment:
      - GATEWAY_PORT=3100
    depends_on:
      - osiris-app
      - websocket-gateway
      - control-plane
    networks: [osiris-net]

  # ─── Observability ────────────────────────────────────────
  prometheus:
    image: prom/prometheus:v2.48.0
    ports:
      - "9090:9090"
    volumes:
      - ./infra/prometheus/prometheus.yml:/etc/prometheus/prometheus.yml:ro
      - prometheus-data:/prometheus
    networks: [osiris-net]

  loki:
    image: grafana/loki:2.9.3
    ports:
      - "3100:3100"
    volumes:
      - ./infra/loki/loki-config.yml:/etc/loki/local-config.yml:ro
      - loki-data:/loki
    networks: [osiris-net]

  tempo:
    image: grafana/tempo:2.2.0
    ports:
      - "3200:3200"
    volumes:
      - ./infra/tempo/tempo.yml:/etc/tempo/tempo.yml:ro
      - tempo-data:/tempo
    networks: [osiris-net]

  grafana:
    image: grafana/grafana:10.2.0
    ports:
      - "3000:3000"
    environment:
      - GF_SECURITY_ADMIN_[REDACTED:ENV_VAR]
    volumes:
      - ./infra/grafana/dashboards:/etc/grafana/provisioning/dashboards:ro
      - grafana-data:/var/lib/grafana
    networks: [osiris-net]

  otel-collector:
    image: otel/opentelemetry-collector-contrib:0.88.0
    ports:
      - "4317:4317"
      - "4318:4318"
    volumes:
      - ./infra/otel/otel-collector-config.yml:/etc/otel-collector-config.yml:ro
    networks: [osiris-net]

  alertmanager:
    image: prom/alertmanager:v0.26.0
    ports:
      - "9093:9093"
    volumes:
      - ./infra/alertmanager/alertmanager.yml:/etc/alertmanager/alertmanager.yml:ro
    networks: [osiris-net]
```

## 6. RÉSUMÉ

### 6.1 Principes Architecturaux

- **Event-Driven First**: Toute communication via Event Bus (Redpanda)
- **Zero Redundancy**: Un seul frontend, un seul Event Bus, un seul Graph DB
- **Single Source of Truth**: Graph DB (Memgraph) + Event Bus (Redpanda)
- **Microservices**: 11 services métier, chacun responsable d'un domaine
- **Scalabilité**: Partitionnement par workspace_id, scaling horizontal par service
- **Résilience**: DLQ, retry, circuit breaker, cache, replication
- **Observabilité**: 100% instrumenté (metrics + logs + traces)

### 6.2 Performance Cibles

| Métrique | Cible |
|----------|-------|
| Ingestion → Graph | < 50ms (P99) |
| Graph Query | < 100ms (P99) |
| AI Prediction | < 200ms (P99) |
| Alert → UI | < 50ms (P99) |
| End-to-End | < 400ms (P99) |
| Event Bus Throughput | 100K events/sec |
| Graph Nodes | 10M+ |
| Concurrent Users | 10K+ |

### 6.3 Évolutivité

- **Horizontal**: Ajouter instances de services (stateless)
- **Partitionnement**: Kafka partitions par workspace_id
- **Sharding**: Memgraph clustering (futur)
- **CDN**: Frontend static assets
- **Cache**: Redis cluster (futur)

### 6.4 Sécurité

- Zero Trust Network (pas d'exposition directe)
- mTLS entre services
- JWT + RBAC
- Audit log (Event Bus)
- Secrets management (Vault)

---

**Architecture terminée. Prête pour implémentation.**

> ⚠️ Secrets filtrés : [REDACTED:ENV_VAR]

## Cartographie complète du système Osiris-Lab v2

### Frontend (Next.js - src/)
- **Components**: EntityGraphPanel, OsirisMap, LayerPanel, IntelFeed, OsintPanel, GeointIntelligencePanel, AiAnalyst, LiveAlerts, etc.
- **API Routes**: ~40+ endpoints (flights, satellites, earthquakes, malware, osint/*, entity/expand, etc.)
- **Libraries**: plugin-engine, intelligence-engine, ai-engine, event-stream, module-registry, ssrf-guard, etc.
- **Digital Twin**: digitalTwinEngine, entitySimulationLayer, environmentModeler, eventReplayEngine

### Services (Go backend)
- **services/kernel**: Graph Engine, GEOINT Engine, IOC Engine, Threat Engine, Search Engine, Workflow Engine, Event Bus, Gateway, MCP, Auth
- **services/ai-engine**: Agent reasoning, RAG pipeline, MCP tools, Self-improvement, Task decomposition
- **services/core-brain**: Stores (Memgraph, ClickHouse, Qdrant, OpenSearch, Postgres, Redis), Plugin manager, Event Bus (kafka/websocket/inprocess), Pipeline validator
- **services/meta-orchestration**: Orchestrator
- **services/event-bus, sensors, tracking-service, osiris-agent, osiris-brain**: existent mais peu de détails

### Workers (Go)
- ai, graph, geoint, threat, orchestrator, cp-engine, digitaltwin, incident-replay, policy-engine, predictive, reward-engine, selfhealing, selfrewrite

### Control Plane (Python/TypeScript)
- bootstrap, event-bus, gateway, security, registry, observability
- Adapters: orphan-adapter-health-scoring, orphan-adapter-realtime-events
- Config: broadcaster, loader, schema, watcher

### Gateway
- Nginx gateway avec routing

### Intel Service
- Node.js /resolve endpoint

### Modules
- Docker module system (health-monitor, module-worker)

### Docker Compose
- Services définis: traefik, gateway, cp-engine, data stores (postgres, memgraph, clickhouse, opensearch, qdrant, redis), ollama, workers (ai, graph, threat, geoint, etc.), observability (prometheus, grafana, loki, tempo)

Let me verify the state of key modules by checking if they have proper entrypoints, imports that resolve, etc.

### 12. PROBLÈMES IDENTIFIÉS

1. **Double frontend Next.js** : `/src/` (racine) vs `/osiris/src/` — deux repos Git distincts
2. **gRPC Server cassé** : proto manquant
3. **WebSocket Gateway cassé** : dépendance manquante
4. **workers/geint/** : probablement une faute d'orthographe de geoint
5. **Binaires compilés orphelins** : 7 binaires standalone non connectés
6. **Control Plane orphan adapters** : signals d'architecture non finie
7. **services/event-bus/ (Node.js)** : existe mais l'event bus utilisé est celui du kernel (Go/Redis)
